([thitinan147/jtek-skill](https://github.com/thitinan147/jtek-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-harness) · ★2)
- **jt-learn**: Turns a correction from the human into something that runs (a lint rule, a fix to the code that taught the wrong pattern, or a new check) instead of a written reminder. Use when the user corrects the agent's work, sends a ticket back from review, or says a mistake keeps happening. ([thitinan147/jtek-skill](https://github.com/thitinan147/jtek-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-learn) · ★2)
- **jt-review**: Reviews one ticket's change as a session that did not write it and records a PASS, PASS+NOTES, or FAIL verdict for the current commit. Use when the user invokes /jt-review with a ticket id, or when a jt-work result says needs-review. ([thitinan147/jtek-skill](https://github.com/thitinan147/jtek-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-review) · ★2)
- **jt-ship**: Pushes a ticket's branch, opens a pull request that shows how each acceptance item was seen working, and moves the ticket to Review without merging. Use when jt-work reaches the Ship step, or when the user asks to open the pull request for a finished ticket. ([thitinan147/jtek-skill](https://github.com/thitinan147/jtek-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-ship) · ★2)
- **jt-triage**: Use jt (jevotron) to triage large data files for semantic anomalies, inconsistent fields, or cases needing review. Preview a few records, scan with task-specific guidance, inspect a bounded shortlist, and iteratively refine the assessment instead of reading or repeatedly searching the entire dataset into context. Use for CSV, JSONL, JSON, YAML, OBO, logs, and other supported data formats; exact lookups and deterministic counts are better handled directly. ([ai4curation/jevotron](https://github.com/ai4curation/jevotron/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-triage) · ★13)
- **jt-understand**: Reads a ticket and the code it touches before any edit, and names how each acceptance item will be seen working for real. Use when jt-work reaches the Understand step, or when the user asks what a ticket needs or how a change should be verified before coding starts. ([thitinan147/jtek-skill](https://github.com/thitinan147/jtek-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-understand) · ★2)
- **jt-verify**: Runs the changed feature the way a user would and looks at the real result, so that passing checks are not mistaken for working software. Use when jt-work reaches the See-it-work step, or when the user asks to confirm that a change actually works in the running app, API, or command-line tool. ([thitinan147/jtek-skill](https://github.com/thitinan147/jtek-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-verify) · ★2)
- **jt-work**: Works a ticket from start to a pull request in its own git worktree by running each step's skill in order, then stops for the human to review and merge. Use when the user invokes /jt-work, names one or more ticket ids or Notion ticket links to implement, or when another agent hands over tickets to work. ([thitinan147/jtek-skill](https://github.com/thitinan147/jtek-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/jt-work) · ★2)

- **jtbd-analysis**: Teaches how to write the Jobs To Be Done (JTBD) analysis artifact in Ideate: one solution-free job statement per core job (Ulwick: verb + object + contextual clarifier) as the stable anchor, with job stories beneath it, one per situation (Klement / Intercom: When…, I want to…, so I can…); the job spec (functional, emotional and social dimensions, obstacles, purchase and use experiences); what gets fired, including non-consumption; Big Hire vs Little Hire; the four forces of progress (push, pull, anxiety, habit); and desired-outcome statements ranked by opportunity (compare-and-contrast at 3–5… ([shafibabar/SDLC-Artifact-Factory](https://github.com/shafibabar/SDLC-Artifact-Factory/tree/HEAD/skills/jtbd-analysis) · ★3)
